# Survival: the complete visual atlas

**299 entries.** Every game below links its exact imagery, video source, and confirmed distribution pages. The wiki displays artwork and video links; the interactive app supplies in-page players.

[Recommendations](Survival) | [Interactive players](https://herbertofury.github.io/Games/#hub=Survival) | [Portable HTML](https://github.com/Herbertofury/Games/releases/latest)

**[ZERO Sievert](Game-1782120-ZERO-Sievert)**

9/10 recommendation | Released | Oct 23, 2024

Now with over 600,000 copies sold, ZERO Sievert is a tense top-down extraction shooter that challenges you to scavenge a procedurally-generated wasteland, loot gear, and explore what’s left of a devastated world. When the odds are stacked against you, you’ll need to do more than just survive...

**[System Arrival](Game-5019800-System-Arrival)**
